      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "recipe_type",
          "id": "tfc:clay_knapping",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Clay Knapping Recipe",
          "key": "recipe-category-lang:tfc.jei.clay_knapping",
          "label": "Clay Knapping Recipe",
          "recipe_type": "tfc:clay_knapping",
          "count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Clay is an incredibly useful and balanced material which can be used for pottery. It can prove challenging to locate at first. Clay is usually hidden by grass, but it is often found in two locations. In areas with at least 175mm Annual Rainfall, clay can be found in patches all over the place, usually marked by the presence of certain Plants.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Clay Indicators",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.name",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Clay, with one of the plants that may indicate its presence.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Athyrium Fern, Canna, Goldenrod, Pampas Grass, Perovskia, and Water Canna all indicate the presence of clay nearby. Clay can also be found in smaller deposits close to water sources, such as rivers, lakes, or ponds. Like with rocks, clay can be knapped to form new items. It requires five clay in your hand to knap. Unlike rocks, if you make a mistake, you can simply close the knapping interface, reshape your clay, and try again.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The Knapping Interface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The small vessel is one such item. Like all pottery items, it must be fired before it can be used. Firing is a process of heating the item up to a point where the clay will turn into a hard Ceramic material, which requires heating to 1400 °C, or Yellow White. In order to do this in the early game, you will need to use a Pit Kiln.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Small Vessel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Knapping a Clay Small Vessel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Another useful pottery item is the Jug. It can be used to pick up and drink fluids, such as fresh water. In order to use it, simply the jug on the fluid in the world. Then use the jug in order to drink from it. The jug can hold 100 mB of fluid at a time.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Knapping a Clay Jug.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Clay is also necessary for making Molds. Molds can have molten metal poured into them, which will eventually solidify into the shape of a mold. The item and potentially the mold can then be retrieved by using on the mold. The most simple type of mold is the ingot mold, to the right.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Knapping a Clay Ingot Mold.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The mold then needs to be fired, like all clay items, to be usable - likely in a Pit Kiln. Once it is fired, molten metal can be poured in. Once the metal has cooled enough, it can be extracted.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The next few pages show several of the knapping patterns for various tools.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Prospector's Pick is an essential tool for locating large quantities of ore.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Pickaxe! The bread and butter tool for mining.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Saw is a tool which is required in order to craft advanced wooden components like a Workbench along with many other devices like Supports.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Scythe is a tool that can harvest plants and leaves in a 3x3x3 area!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Chisel is a tool used for smoothing blocks as well as creating a large number of decorative blocks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "An Axe for all your tree chopping purposes. Note that stone axes are less efficient than metal!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Hammer is an essential tool to create and work on Anvils.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {

          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Knife can be used as a weapon, or as a cutting tool for plant type blocks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.19.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {

          "text": "A Hoe used for planting and maintaining Crops.",

          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Shovel for all your digging purposes.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.21.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls.json",
          "text": "Bowls are a versatile tool which can be used to make Salads, to make Soups, to Salt meat, or to apply Powder to Glass in order to change the resulting glass's color. Bowls can be made both from Ceramic, by knapping a bowl out of clay, and then firing them.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Bowls",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls.json",
          "text": "Multiple unfired bowls can be made from one knapping.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Bowls",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls.json",
          "text": "Wooden Bowls",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Bowls",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls.json",
          "text": "Bowls can also be made out of wood via crafting them together with Glue Glue can be made by soaking Bone Meal in a barrel of Limewater.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Bowls",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls.json",
          "text": "Bowls can also be placed on the ground like blocks. When placed, they can be filled with any type of Powder - the results of grinding ores, minerals, or gems in the Quern.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Bowls",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls.json",
          "text": "The Powder Bowl can hold up to 16 of a given powder. To insert items, while holding the powder. To extract items, with an empty hand. allows extracting the entire contents of the bowl.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Bowls",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls.json",
          "text": "If there is salt in the bowl, clicking with unsalted raw meat will salt the meat. This is the same as crafting the meat with the salt in your inventory.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Bowls",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Glassworking is the process of turning sand into glass. To start, you must create a Glass Batch, of which there are four types: 1. Silica, from white sand. 2. Hematitic, from yellow, red, or pink sand. 3. Olivine, from green or brown sand. 4. Volcanic, from black sand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Silica Glass Batch",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Glass batches can then be crafted using one of the aforementioned colors of sand, plus Lime and a type of Potash.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Lime is one of the ingredients required to make glass batches. It is a powder obtained by heating Flux.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "A type of Potash or equivalent is also required for glass batches. Soda Ash can be used, which is a powder made from heating Dried Seaweed or Kelp. Saltpeter can be used as well.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Glassworking is done by starting with a glass batch, and then completing a series of steps. These steps may require specific tools: - A Blowpipe, to Blow and Stretch - A Paddle, to Flatten - Jacks, to Pinch - A Gem Saw, to Saw",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Tools of the Trade",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The most important tool is the Blowpipe. It can be knapped from clay, and then fired into a Ceramic Blowpipe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Ceramic blowpipes are brittle, and have a chance to to break when used. A more sturdy blowpipe can be worked from a Brass Rod on an anvil.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The Flatten operation can be done with a Paddle, which is crafted from wood.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The Pinch operation can be done with Jacks, made from welding two brass rods together.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The Saw operation can be done with a Gem Saw. The gem saw is also used to break both Glass Blocks and Glass Panes and obtain them.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "First, glass on the blowpipe must be heated to Faint Red. Then, hold the blowpipe in your offhand and hold to perform each step. Use to pick up a hot blowpipe into your offhand. Blow Use the Blowpipe while facing straight ahead. Stretch Use the Blowpipe while facing straight down.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "How to Glass",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Flatten Use the Blowpipe while holding a Paddle in your main hand. Pinch Use the Blowpipe while holding Jacks in your main hand. Saw Use the Blowpipe while holding a Gem Saw in your main hand. Roll Use the Blowpipe with a Wool Cloth in your main hand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Glassworking is the process of turning sand into glass. To start, you must create a Glass Batch, of which there are four types: 1. Silica, from white sand. 2. Hematitic, from yellow, red, or pink sand. 3. Olivine, from green or brown sand. 4. Volcanic, from black sand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },

          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",

        {

          "text": "Tools of the Trade",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,

          "count": 12
        },
        {

          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",

        {

        },
        {

          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",

          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",

          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The Saw operation can be done with a Gem Saw. The gem saw is also used to break both Glass Blocks and Glass Panes and obtain them.",

          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "First, glass on the blowpipe must be heated to Faint Red.Then, hold the blowpipe and hold to perform each step. Blow Use the Blowpipe while facing straight ahead. Stretch Use the Blowpipe while facing straight down.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",

          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "How to Glass",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Flatten Use the Blowpipe while holding a Paddle in your offhand. Pinch Use the Blowpipe while holding Jacks in your offhand. Saw Use the Blowpipe while holding a Gem Saw in your offhand. Roll Use the Blowpipe with a Wool Cloth in your offhand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/panning",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/panning.json",
          "text": "Panning is a method of obtaining small pieces of certain native ores by searching in rivers and other waterways. Panning makes use of Ore Deposits which are found in gravel patches in the bottom of lakes and rivers. In order to get started panning, you will need an empty pan.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Panning",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/panning",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/panning.json",
          "text": "Clay can be knapped into a pan as shown above.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Panning",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/panning",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/panning.json",
          "text": "Once the pan has been knapped, it needs to be fired to create a Ceramic Pan. The next thing you will need to find is some sort of Ore Deposit. Ore deposits can come in several different ores: Native Copper, Native Silver, Native Gold, and Cassiterite.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Panning",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 7
        }
      ],

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "recipe_type",

          "text": "Clay Knapping Recipe",
          "key": "recipe-category-lang:tfc.jei.clay_knapping",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Clay is an incredibly useful and balanced material which can be used for pottery. It can prove challenging to locate at first. Clay is usually hidden by grass, but it is often found in two locations. In areas with at least 175mm Annual Rainfall, clay can be found in patches all over the place, usually marked by the presence of certain Plants.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Clay Indicators",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.name",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Clay, with one of the plants that may indicate its presence.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Athyrium Fern, Canna, Goldenrod, Pampas Grass, Perovskia, and Water Canna all indicate the presence of clay nearby. Clay can also be found in smaller deposits close to water sources, such as rivers, lakes, or ponds. Like with rocks, clay can be knapped to form new items. It requires five clay in your hand to knap. Unlike rocks, if you make a mistake, you can simply close the knapping interface, reshape your clay, and try again.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The Knapping Interface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The small vessel is one such item. Like all pottery items, it must be fired before it can be used. Firing is a process of heating the item up to a point where the clay will turn into a hard Ceramic material, which requires heating to 1400 °C, or Yellow White. In order to do this in the early game, you will need to use a Pit Kiln.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Small Vessel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Knapping a Clay Small Vessel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Another useful pottery item is the Jug. It can be used to pick up and drink fluids, such as fresh water. In order to use it, simply the jug on the fluid in the world. Then use the jug in order to drink from it. The jug can hold 100 mB of fluid at a time.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Knapping a Clay Jug.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Clay is also necessary for making Molds. Molds can have molten metal poured into them, which will eventually solidify into the shape of a mold. The item and potentially the mold can then be retrieved by using on the mold. The most simple type of mold is the ingot mold, to the right.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Knapping a Clay Ingot Mold.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The mold then needs to be fired, like all clay items, to be usable - likely in a Pit Kiln. Once it is fired, molten metal can be poured in. Once the metal has cooled enough, it can be extracted.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The next few pages show several of the knapping patterns for various tools.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Prospector's Pick is an essential tool for locating large quantities of ore.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Pickaxe! The bread and butter tool for mining.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Saw is a tool which is required in order to craft advanced wooden components like a Workbench along with many other devices like Supports.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Scythe is a tool that can harvest plants and leaves in a 3x3x3 area!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Chisel is a tool used for smoothing blocks as well as creating a large number of decorative blocks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "An Axe for all your tree chopping purposes. Note that stone axes are less efficient than metal!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Hammer is an essential tool to create and work on Anvils.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Knife can be used as a weapon, or as a cutting tool for plant type blocks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.19.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Hoe used for planting and maintaining Crops.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Shovel for all your digging purposes.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.21.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls.json",
          "text": "Bowls are a versatile tool which can be used to make Salads, to make Soups, to Salt meat, or to apply Powder to Glass in order to change the resulting glass's color. Bowls can be made both from Ceramic, by knapping a bowl out of clay, and then firing them.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Bowls",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls.json",
          "text": "Multiple unfired bowls can be made from one knapping.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Bowls",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls.json",
          "text": "Wooden Bowls",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Bowls",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls.json",
          "text": "Bowls can also be made out of wood via crafting them together with Glue Glue can be made by soaking Bone Meal in a barrel of Limewater.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Bowls",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls.json",
          "text": "Bowls can also be placed on the ground like blocks. When placed, they can be filled with any type of Powder - the results of grinding ores, minerals, or gems in the Quern.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Bowls",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls.json",
          "text": "The Powder Bowl can hold up to 16 of a given powder. To insert items, while holding the powder. To extract items, with an empty hand. allows extracting the entire contents of the bowl.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Bowls",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bowls.json",
          "text": "If there is salt in the bowl, clicking with unsalted raw meat will salt the meat. This is the same as crafting the meat with the salt in your inventory.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Bowls",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Glassworking is the process of turning sand into glass. To start, you must create a Glass Batch, of which there are four types: 1. Silica, from white sand. 2. Hematitic, from yellow, red, or pink sand. 3. Olivine, from green or brown sand. 4. Volcanic, from black sand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Silica Glass Batch",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Glass batches can then be crafted using one of the aforementioned colors of sand, plus Lime and a type of Potash.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Lime is one of the ingredients required to make glass batches. It is a powder obtained by heating Flux.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "A type of Potash or equivalent is also required for glass batches. Soda Ash can be used, which is a powder made from heating Dried Seaweed or Kelp. Saltpeter can be used as well.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Glassworking is done by starting with a glass batch, and then completing a series of steps. These steps may require specific tools: - A Blowpipe, to Blow and Stretch - A Paddle, to Flatten - Jacks, to Pinch - A Gem Saw, to Saw",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Tools of the Trade",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The most important tool is the Blowpipe. It can be knapped from clay, and then fired into a Ceramic Blowpipe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Ceramic blowpipes are brittle, and have a chance to to break when used. A more sturdy blowpipe can be worked from a Brass Rod on an anvil.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The Flatten operation can be done with a Paddle, which is crafted from wood.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The Pinch operation can be done with Jacks, made from welding two brass rods together.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The Saw operation can be done with a Gem Saw. The gem saw is also used to break both Glass Blocks and Glass Panes and obtain them.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "First, glass on the blowpipe must be heated to Faint Red. Then, hold the blowpipe in your offhand and hold to perform each step. Use to pick up a hot blowpipe into your offhand. Blow Use the Blowpipe while facing straight ahead. Stretch Use the Blowpipe while facing straight down.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "How to Glass",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Flatten Use the Blowpipe while holding a Paddle in your main hand. Pinch Use the Blowpipe while holding Jacks in your main hand. Saw Use the Blowpipe while holding a Gem Saw in your main hand. Roll Use the Blowpipe with a Wool Cloth in your main hand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Glassworking is the process of turning sand into glass. To start, you must create a Glass Batch, of which there are four types: 1. Silica, from white sand. 2. Hematitic, from yellow, red, or pink sand. 3. Olivine, from green or brown sand. 4. Volcanic, from black sand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Glass batches can then be crafted using one of the aforementioned colors of sand, plus Lime and a type of Potash.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Lime is one of the ingredients required to make glass batches. It is a powder obtained by heating Flux.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "A type of Potash or equivalent is also required for glass batches. Soda Ash can be used, which is a powder made from heating Dried Seaweed or Kelp. Saltpeter can be used as well.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Glassworking is done by starting with a glass batch, and then completing a series of steps. These steps may require specific tools: - A Blowpipe, to Blow and Stretch - A Paddle, to Flatten - Jacks, to Pinch - A Gem Saw, to Saw",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Tools of the Trade",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "The most important tool is the Blowpipe. It can be knapped from clay, and then fired into a Ceramic Blowpipe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {

          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",

          "count": 12
        },

          "count": 12
        },

        {

          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",

          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking.json",
          "text": "Flatten Use the Blowpipe while holding a Paddle in your offhand. Pinch Use the Blowpipe while holding Jacks in your offhand. Saw Use the Blowpipe while holding a Gem Saw in your offhand. Roll Use the Blowpipe with a Wool Cloth in your offhand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Glassworking",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/panning",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/panning.json",
          "text": "Panning is a method of obtaining small pieces of certain native ores by searching in rivers and other waterways. Panning makes use of Ore Deposits which are found in gravel patches in the bottom of lakes and rivers. In order to get started panning, you will need an empty pan.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Panning",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/panning",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/panning.json",
          "text": "Clay can be knapped into a pan as shown above.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Panning",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/panning",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/panning.json",
          "text": "Once the pan has been knapped, it needs to be fired to create a Ceramic Pan. The next thing you will need to find is some sort of Ore Deposit. Ore deposits can come in several different ores: Native Copper, Native Silver, Native Gold, and Cassiterite.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Panning",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 7
        }

    {
      "id": "tfc:rock_knapping#input",
      "label": "Rock Knapping Recipe Input",
      "origin": "pack_generated",
      "confidence": 0.65,
      "support": 2,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "recipe_type",
          "id": "tfc:rock_knapping",
          "confidence": 0.65
        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "recipe_type",
          "id": "tfc:rock_knapping",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Rock Knapping Recipe",
          "key": "recipe-category-lang:tfc.jei.rock_knapping",
          "label": "Rock Knapping Recipe",
          "recipe_type": "tfc:rock_knapping",
          "count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "In TerraFirmaCraft, the first things you can obtain are sticks, twigs, and loose rocks. They can be found in almost every climate, lying scattered on the ground. or break these to pick them up.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "A smattering of common sticks and stones.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "In addition to gathering sticks and twigs on the ground, sticks can also be obtained by breaking leaves with your fist. Once you have a number of rocks and sticks, you are ready to start Knapping. Knapping is a process where two rocks are hit together, to form a particular shape. In order to knap, first hold at least two rocks in your hand, then right click in the air, which will open up the Knapping Interface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "The Knapping Interface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "In order to knap a particular item, you want to remove squares until you form the desired pattern. For example, create a knife blade by matching the recipe shown to the right. Like crafting recipes, the location of the desired pattern doesn't matter for the output, and some recipes have multiple variants that are valid.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "A knife blade, crafted from several different rock types.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "All stone tool heads can be crafted with a stick or twig to assemble them into a tool. The stone knife can be used to harvest Straw by breaking plants.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "Axes can be used to cut down entire trees, logs and leaves included. However, saplings and sticks will only be obtained if leaves were broken individually.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "Shovels can be used to dig soil type blocks. They can also be used to create Paths by using them on Dirt or Grass.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "Hoes are useful tools for Farming, but they can also be used to cut down leaves and other plant matter.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "Hammers can be used as Crushing weapons, but are more important tools used for Forging.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "Finally, a Javelin can be used as a primitive weapon, capable of being thrown at targets, or used as a Piercing weapon.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",

          "text": "In TerraFirmaCraft, the first things you can obtain are sticks, twigs, and loose rocks. They can be found in almost every climate, lying scattered on the ground. or break these to pick them up.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 25,
          "count": 12
        },

        },
        {
